00036: def B(owner, slot, size, center, color):
00037:     return owner.include(slot, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00038: 
00039: def R(owner, slot, radius, start, end, color):
00040:     return owner.include(slot, rod(radius=radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))
00041: 
00042: def E(owner, slot, center, radii, color):
00043:     node = ball(1, center=center, color=color)
00044:     node.resize(radii, pivot=center)
00045:     return owner.include(slot, node)
00046: 
00047: def tube(owner, slot, points, radius, color):
00048:     return owner.include(slot, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))
00049: 
00050: def ring(owner, slot, center, rx, rz, radius, color, count=28):
00051:     x,y,z = center
00052:     pts = [(x + rx*math.cos(2*math.pi*i/count), y,
00053:             z + rz*math.sin(2*math.pi*i/count)) for i in range(count+1)]
00054:     return tube(owner, slot, pts, radius, color)

00216: # Pre-execution self-review:
00217: # All expected inventory has an editable semantic owner. Counter contacts use z=.90,
00218: # not a bounding-box top including backsplash. Cabinet and ring mount to the rear wall.
00219: # Cloth shape, jug handle, pitcher transparency and wall/window layout are uncertain.
00220: # ~250 primitive meshes; blinds and two curved loops are largest repeated families.
00221: # No fillet boxes; positive finite sizes; all adjacent tube points distinct.
00222: # Repeated slots include indices. No geometry is created outside SceneAct after realize.
00223: # Camera uses upper cabinet, lower sink crop and far window/ring as independent anchors.
00224: # Material edits below traverse exact semantic owners and assert nonempty descendants.
00225: # This is a code review, not a claim of measured contact or regression success.
00226: realize(scene)
00227: 
00228: def meshes_under(slot):
00229:     obj=bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00230:     assert obj is not None, slot
00231:     meshes=[x for x in obj.children_recursive if x.type=='MESH']
00232:     if obj.type=='MESH':
00233:         meshes.append(obj)
00234:     assert meshes, slot
00235:     return meshes
00236: 
00237: # Counter-only procedural grain; reference photograph is never used as a texture.
00238: for obj in meshes_under('architecture'):
00239:     if obj.parent and obj.parent.name in {'counter_slab','backsplash','counter_front'}:
00240:         obj.data=obj.data.copy()
00241:         for index, old in enumerate(list(obj.data.materials)):
00242:             if old is None:
00243:                 continue
00244:             mat=old.copy()
00245:             obj.data.materials[index]=mat
00246:             mat.use_nodes=True
00247:             nt=mat.node_tree
00248:             bs=nt.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00249:             noise=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00250:             noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value=175
00251:             noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value=2
00252:             ramp=nt.nodes.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
00253:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.26
00254:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=(.075,.065,.043,1)
00255:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.73
00256:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=(.47,.43,.32,1)
00257:             nt.links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'],ramp.inputs['Fac'])
00258:             nt.links.new(ramp.outputs['Color'],bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00259:             bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.72
00260: 
00261: for slot in ('faucet','sink','towel','paper_towels'):
00262:     for obj in meshes_under(slot):
00263:         for old in list(obj.data.materials):
00264:             if old is None:
00265:                 continue
00266:             # Leave each material family local to the selected owner.
00267:             pass

00048: def B(owner, slot, size, center, color):
00049:     return owner.include(slot, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00050: 
00051: def R(owner, slot, radius, start, end, color):
00052:     return owner.include(slot, rod(radius=radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))
00053: 
00054: def E(owner, slot, center, radii, color):
00055:     node = ball(1, center=center, color=color)
00056:     node.resize(radii, pivot=center)
00057:     return owner.include(slot, node)
00058: 
00059: def tube(owner, slot, points, radius, color):
00060:     return owner.include(slot, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))
00061: 
00062: def ring(owner, slot, center, rx, rz, radius, color, count=28):
00063:     x, y, z = center
00064:     pts = [(x + rx*math.cos(2*math.pi*i/count), y,
00065:             z + rz*math.sin(2*math.pi*i/count)) for i in range(count+1)]
00066:     return tube(owner, slot, pts, radius, color)

00059: def B(owner, slot, size, center, color):
00060:     return owner.include(slot, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00061: 
00062: def R(owner, slot, radius, start, end, color):
00063:     return owner.include(slot, rod(radius=radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))
00064: 
00065: def E(owner, slot, center, radii, color):
00066:     node = ball(1, center=center, color=color)
00067:     node.resize(radii, pivot=center)
00068:     return owner.include(slot, node)
00069: 
00070: def tube(owner, slot, points, radius, color):
00071:     return owner.include(slot, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))
00072: 
00073: def ring(owner, slot, center, rx, rz, radius, color, count=28):
00074:     x, y, z = center
00075:     points = [(x + rx*math.cos(2*math.pi*i/count), y,
00076:                z + rz*math.sin(2*math.pi*i/count)) for i in range(count+1)]
00077:     return tube(owner, slot, points, radius, color)

00237: # Inline pre-execution self-review:
00238: # Inventory, complete owners, dimensions, support and transforms preserved.
00239: # Counter-supported bodies use z=.90; fascia/backsplash are not support anchors.
00240: # About 250 expanded primitives; 35 slats and 28-segment ring are main repeats.
00241: # Tubes expand to rods/balls; no dense textile or grain geometry is introduced.
00242: # Positive finite dimensions; no fillet radii; adjacent tube endpoints distinct.
00243: # Every repeated include slot is indexed and unique within its parent.
00244: # All geometry remains in SceneAct; realize precedes camera/world/lights.
00245: # Counter material changes use exact nonempty component descendants and copies.
00246: # Other material edits are explicit palette choices in their original builders.
00247: # Shader code preserves owner transforms and mesh topology.
00248: # Review is not a claim of collision accuracy or passing regression tests.
00249: realize(scene)
00250: 
00251: def meshes_under(slot):
00252:     obj=bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00253:     assert obj is not None, slot
00254:     meshes=[x for x in obj.children_recursive if x.type=='MESH']
00255:     if obj.type=='MESH':
00256:         meshes.append(obj)
00257:     assert meshes, slot
00258:     return meshes
00259: 
00260: # Procedural granite is restricted to the three counter components.
00261: # No input image or external texture is used.

00173: # Inventory, supports, owners and uncertain occlusions are recorded above.
00174: # Hood is the sole changed owner; its filter fits within the reduced enclosure,
00175: # lip and switch track its dimensions, and upper end meets ceiling underside.
00176: # Camera anchors stay fixed: near post contact, island contact/front corner,
00177: # ceiling beam slopes and distant oven. Absolute scale remains unknown.
00178: # Approximately 110 primitives; major repetition is box beams/cabinet divisions.
00179: # Curved faucet and kettle handle expand to 7 and 9 primitives respectively.
00180: # Positive finite dimensions, unique include slots, no fillets, distinct rod
00181: # endpoints and adjacent sweep points. No geometry is added after realize.
00182: # Material scopes below assert nonempty descendants, create local materials
00183: # and copy mesh data before replacing slots. Non-target assignments stay fixed.
00184: # This is an inline code review, not a claim of regression-test success.
00185: realize(scene)
00186: 
00187: def descendants(slot):
00188:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00189:     assert marker is not None, "Missing semantic marker: " + slot
00190:     meshes = [o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00191:     if marker.type == 'MESH':
00192:         meshes.append(marker)
00193:     assert meshes, "Empty material scope: " + slot
00194:     return meshes
00195: 
00196: def scoped_material(slot,name,color,roughness=.6,metallic=0.0,texture=None):
00197:     meshes = descendants(slot)
00198:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00199:     mat.use_nodes = True
00200:     bs = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00201:     bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color,1)
00202:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00203:     bs.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00204:     if texture:
00205:         nodes,links = mat.node_tree.nodes,mat.node_tree.links
00206:         tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00207:         tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = texture["scale"]
00208:         tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00209:         coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00210:         mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00211:         mapping.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00212:         mapping.inputs[1].default_value = texture["stretch"]
00213:         links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],mapping.inputs[0])
00214:         links.new(mapping.outputs[0],tex.inputs["Vector"])
00215:         ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00216:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .20
00217:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (*texture["dark"],1)
00218:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .80
00219:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*texture["light"],1)
00220:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00221:         links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00222:         bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00223:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .15
00224:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .008
00225:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00226:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])
00227:     for obj in meshes:
00228:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00229:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00230:         obj.data.materials.append(mat)

00190: # All major inventory has owners and explicit supports. Island, cupboards and

00192: # Camera anchors: near full-height timber crop at left; island end/front junction
00193: # near (237,321); far oven near (280,250); hood lower edge around y220.
00194: # Perspective and hidden depths remain uncertain; no calibration is asserted.
00195: # Roughly 110 boxes/balls/rods plus 16 tube primitives. Repeated decoration is
00196: # limited to ceiling beams, sparse tile joints and cabinet divisions.
00197: # Dimensions positive, rods distinct, no fillets, unique include keys.
00198: # Realize precedes all camera/world/light and material work below.
00199: realize(scene)

00201: # Scoped shader helpers resolve semantic EMPTY markers and their descendants.
00202: def descendants(slot):
00203:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00204:     assert marker is not None, "Missing semantic marker: " + slot
00205:     meshes = [o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00206:     if marker.type == 'MESH':
00207:         meshes.append(marker)
00208:     assert meshes, "Empty material scope: " + slot
00209:     return meshes
00210: 
00211: def scoped_material(slot, name, color, roughness=.6, metallic=0.0, texture=None):
00212:     meshes = descendants(slot)
00213:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00214:     mat.use_nodes = True
00215:     bs = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00216:     bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color,1)
00217:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00218:     bs.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00219:     if texture:
00220:         nodes, links = mat.node_tree.nodes, mat.node_tree.links
00221:         tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00222:         tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = texture["scale"]
00223:         tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00224:         coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00225:         mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00226:         mapping.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00227:         mapping.inputs[1].default_value = texture["stretch"]
00228:         links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],mapping.inputs[0])
00229:         links.new(mapping.outputs[0],tex.inputs["Vector"])
00230:         ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00231:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .20
00232:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (*texture["dark"],1)
00233:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .80
00234:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*texture["light"],1)
00235:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00236:         links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00237:         bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00238:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .15
00239:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .008
00240:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00241:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])
00242:     for obj in meshes:
00243:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00244:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00245:         obj.data.materials.append(mat)
00246: 
00247: scoped_material("timber_posts", "Warm longitudinal timber",
00248:                 PALETTE["wood"], .68, texture={
00249:                     "scale":5, "stretch":(3.5,3.5,.15),
00250:                     "dark":(.22,.105,.038), "light":(.56,.37,.19)})

00253:                          "dark":(.66,.65,.57),"light":(.83,.82,.74)})

00210: def descendants(slot):
00211:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00212:     assert marker is not None, "Missing semantic marker: " + slot
00213:     meshes = [o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00214:     if marker.type == 'MESH':
00215:         meshes.append(marker)
00216:     assert meshes, "Empty material scope: " + slot
00217:     return meshes
00218: 
00219: def scoped_material(slot, name, color, roughness=.6, metallic=0.0, texture=None):
00220:     meshes = descendants(slot)
00221:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00222:     mat.use_nodes = True
00223:     bs = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00224:     bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color,1)
00225:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00226:     bs.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00227:     if texture:
00228:         nodes, links = mat.node_tree.nodes, mat.node_tree.links
00229:         tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00230:         tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = texture["scale"]
00231:         tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00232:         coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00233:         mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00234:         mapping.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00235:         mapping.inputs[1].default_value = texture["stretch"]
00236:         links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],mapping.inputs[0])
00237:         links.new(mapping.outputs[0],tex.inputs["Vector"])
00238:         ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00239:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .20
00240:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (*texture["dark"],1)
00241:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .80
00242:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*texture["light"],1)
00243:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00244:         links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00245:         bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00246:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .15
00247:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .008
00248:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00249:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])
00250:     for obj in meshes:
00251:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00252:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00253:         obj.data.materials.append(mat)

00187: def descendants(slot):
00188:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00189:     assert marker is not None, "Missing semantic marker: " + slot
00190:     meshes = [o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00191:     if marker.type == 'MESH':
00192:         meshes.append(marker)
00193:     assert meshes, "Empty material scope: " + slot
00194:     return meshes
00195: 
00196: def scoped_material(slot,name,color,roughness=.6,metallic=0.0,texture=None):
00197:     meshes = descendants(slot)
00198:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00199:     mat.use_nodes = True
00200:     bs = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00201:     bs.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color,1)
00202:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00203:     bs.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00204:     if texture:
00205:         nodes,links = mat.node_tree.nodes,mat.node_tree.links
00206:         tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00207:         tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = texture["scale"]
00208:         tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00209:         coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00210:         mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00211:         mapping.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00212:         mapping.inputs[1].default_value = texture["stretch"]
00213:         links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],mapping.inputs[0])
00214:         links.new(mapping.outputs[0],tex.inputs["Vector"])
00215:         ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00216:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .20
00217:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (*texture["dark"],1)
00218:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .80
00219:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*texture["light"],1)
00220:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00221:         links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00222:         bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00223:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .15
00224:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .008
00225:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00226:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])
00227:     for obj in meshes:
00228:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00229:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00230:         obj.data.materials.append(mat)

00058: def cube(owner, name, size, pos, col):
00059:     return owner.include(name, box(size, center=pos, color=col))
00060: 
00061: def bar(owner, name, a, b, r, col=IRON):
00062:     return owner.include(name, rod(radius=r, start=a, end=b, color=col))
00063: 
00064: def ring(owner, name, center, radius, tube, color, segments=24, plane="xz"):
00065:     p = Part(name)
00066:     cx, cy, cz = center
00067:     pts = []
00068:     for i in range(segments):
00069:         t = 2 * math.pi * i / segments
00070:         if plane == "xz":
00071:             pts.append((cx + radius*math.cos(t), cy, cz + radius*math.sin(t)))
00072:         elif plane == "yz":
00073:             pts.append((cx, cy + radius*math.cos(t), cz + radius*math.sin(t)))
00074:         else:
00075:             pts.append((cx + radius*math.cos(t), cy + radius*math.sin(t), cz))
00076:     for i in range(segments):
00077:         bar(p, "rim_%02d" % i, pts[i], pts[(i+1) % segments], tube, color)
00078:     return owner.include(name, p)
00079: 
00080: def wheel(owner, name, center, radius, color, spokes=5):
00081:     p = Part(name)
00082:     x, y, z = center
00083:     ring(p, "outer", center, radius, .025, color)
00084:     ring(p, "inner_edge", (x, y+.022, z), radius-.035, .011, WORN)
00085:     bar(p, "hub", (x, y-.065, z), (x, y+.075, z), .068, color)
00086:     for i in range(spokes):
00087:         a = 2*math.pi*i/spokes + .2
00088:         b = a + .20
00089:         start = (x+.055*math.cos(a), y, z+.055*math.sin(a))
00090:         bend = (x+radius*.57*math.cos(b), y, z+radius*.57*math.sin(b))
00091:         end = (x+(radius-.025)*math.cos(a+.12), y,
00092:                z+(radius-.025)*math.sin(a+.12))
00093:         bar(p, "spoke_%d_a" % i, start, bend, .024, color)
00094:         bar(p, "spoke_%d_b" % i, bend, end, .024, color)
00095:     bar(p, "crank_pin", (x+radius*.68, y, z-.08),
00096:         (x+radius*.68, y-.13, z-.08), .024, WORN)
00097:     return owner.include(name, p)

00311: # Inline pre-execution self-review:
00312: # Inventory, geometry, actual support coordinates and owner transforms retained.
00313: # Every include loop uses unique slots; returned lathe owners carry their wheels.
00314: # Wheel negative space, open drawer, foreground occluders and wall returns retained.
00315: # Roughly 700 primitive meshes; wheel rim/spoke rods dominate repeated decoration,
00316: # followed by 43 floorboards and two 16-segment container walls. No fillet expansion.
00317: # Positive finite dimensions/radii; distinct rod endpoints; no cyclic dependencies.
00318: # Physical support accuracy remains approximate, not certified by successful execution.
00319: # Realize precedes camera/world/lights. No post-realize geometry creation.
00320: # Nonempty semantic shader descendants are asserted; copies preserve local scope.
00321: # Only world strength and emitter powers intentionally differ from step 2.
00322: # Fixed runner render settings, output paths and execution limits are untouched.

00325: 
00326: def mesh_descendants(marker_name):
00327:     root = bpy.data.objects.get(marker_name)
00328:     if root is None:
00329:         candidates = [o for o in bpy.data.objects
00330:                       if o.type == "EMPTY" and o.name.split(".")[0] == marker_name]
00331:         assert candidates, marker_name
00332:         root = candidates[0]
00333:     meshes = [o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type == "MESH"]
00334:     assert meshes, marker_name
00335:     return meshes
00336: 
00337: def add_surface_variation(marker_name, scale, strength):
00338:     for ob in mesh_descendants(marker_name):

00340:         for idx, mat in enumerate(ob.data.materials):

00342:                 continue

00349:                 continue
00350:             bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .87

00034: def cube(owner, name, size, pos, col):
00035:     return owner.include(name, box(size, center=pos, color=col))

00037: def bar(owner, name, a, b, r, col=IRON):

00043: def ring(owner, name, center, radius, tube, color, segments=24, plane="xz"):
00044:     p = Part(name)
00045:     cx, cy, cz = center
00046:     pts = []
00047:     for i in range(segments):
00048:         t = 2*math.pi*i/segments
00049:         if plane == "xz":
00050:             pts.append((cx+radius*math.cos(t), cy, cz+radius*math.sin(t)))
00051:         elif plane == "yz":
00052:             pts.append((cx, cy+radius*math.cos(t), cz+radius*math.sin(t)))
00053:         else:
00054:             pts.append((cx+radius*math.cos(t), cy+radius*math.sin(t), cz))
00055:     for i in range(segments):
00056:         bar(p, "rim_%02d" % i, pts[i], pts[(i+1) % segments], tube, color)
00057:     return owner.include(name, p)
00058: 
00059: def wheel(owner, name, center, radius, color, spokes=5):
00060:     # All rim, spokes, hub and crank belong to the wheel's machine owner.
00061:     p = Part(name)
00062:     x,y,z = center
00063:     ring(p, "outer", center, radius, .025, color)
00064:     ring(p, "inner_edge", (x,y+.022,z), radius-.035, .011, WORN)
00065:     bar(p, "hub", (x,y-.065,z), (x,y+.075,z), .068, color)
00066:     for i in range(spokes):
00067:         a = 2*math.pi*i/spokes + .2
00068:         b = a+.20
00069:         start = (x+.055*math.cos(a), y, z+.055*math.sin(a))
00070:         bend = (x+radius*.57*math.cos(b), y, z+radius*.57*math.sin(b))
00071:         end = (x+(radius-.025)*math.cos(a+.12), y, z+(radius-.025)*math.sin(a+.12))
00072:         bar(p, "spoke_%d_a" % i, start, bend, .024, color)
00073:         bar(p, "spoke_%d_b" % i, bend, end, .024, color)
00074:     bar(p, "crank_pin", (x+radius*.68,y,z-.08),
00075:         (x+radius*.68,y-.13,z-.08), .024, WORN)
00076:     return owner.include(name, p)

00278: # sill vessels start z=1.86; bins and bench posts meet floor at z~0.

00280: # open drawer below foreground bed. Wheel planes and far machine identities remain uncertain.
00281: # Approx. 650 economical primitives, dominated by floorboards, five wheel rims/spokes,
00282: # container walls and machinery. No fillet expansion or densely sampled straight tubes.
00283: # All dimensions/radii positive, rod endpoints distinct, include names unique within parents.

00285: # Scoped shader traversal below asserts nonempty mesh descendants and copies materials.
00286: 
00287: realize(scene)
00288: 
00289: def mesh_descendants(marker_name):
00290:     root=bpy.data.objects.get(marker_name)
00291:     if root is None:
00292:         candidates=[o for o in bpy.data.objects if o.type=="EMPTY" and o.name.split(".")[0]==marker_name]
00293:         assert candidates, marker_name
00294:         root=candidates[0]
00295:     meshes=[o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type=="MESH"]
00296:     assert meshes, marker_name
00297:     return meshes
00298: 
00299: # Broad, low-amplitude surface variation belongs to architecture and bench only.
00300: def add_surface_variation(marker_name, scale, strength):
00301:     for ob in mesh_descendants(marker_name):
00302:         ob.data=ob.data.copy()
00303:         for idx, mat in enumerate(ob.data.materials):
00304:             if mat is None:
00305:                 continue
00306:             mat=mat.copy()
00307:             ob.data.materials[idx]=mat
00308:             mat.use_nodes=True
00309:             nt=mat.node_tree
00310:             bs=nt.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00311:             if bs is None:
00312:                 continue
00313:             bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.87
00314:             tex=nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00315:             tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value=scale
00316:             tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value=2
00317:             bump=nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00318:             bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value=strength
00319:             bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value=.035
00320:             nt.links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00321:             nt.links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])
00322: 
00323: add_surface_variation("architecture",7,.28)
00324: add_surface_variation("workbench",18,.34)
